# Customer account notes

Karen's notes for the order desk. Kept in Drive. Last properly tidied March 2026. Prices are ex VAT unless it says otherwise.

**Price bands:** List = full price list. **B** = 5% off list. **T** = 10% off list. The band is set on the account in Orbit. Contract deals below override the band for the items listed.

Karen's notes for the order desk. Kept in Drive. Last properly tidied March 2026. Prices are ex VAT unless it says otherwise.

**Price bands:** List = full price list. **B** = 5% off list. **T** = 10% off list. The band is set on the account in Orbit. Contract deals below override the band for the items listed.
